     5→
     6→# NestJS Skill
     7→
     8→Comprehensive guide for developing, debugging, and optimizing NestJS TypeScript applications following project-specific patterns.
     9→
    10→## When to Use
    11→
    12→Trigger this skill for ALL code changes in this NestJS repository:
    13→- Creating components (modules, services, controllers, guards, pipes, decorators, interceptors)
    14→- Updating existing logic
    15→- Implementing new features
    16→- Database operations
    17→- Performance optimization
    18→- Architecture decisions
    19→- Debugging NestJS-specific issues
    20→
    21→## Critical Project Patterns
    22→
    23→**ALWAYS reference CLAUDE.md for complete patterns.** Key requirements:
    24→
    25→### Code Style
    26→- **NO COMMENTS** in code (except ESLint disable)
    27→- **NO EXPLANATIONS** in code
    28→- **INSPECT existing files** before creating new ones
    29→- Match exact patterns from similar files
    30→
    31→### Database Pattern (CRITICAL)
    32→
    33→```typescript
    34→constructor(
    35→  @InjectConnection(KNEX_READ_CONNECTION) private readonly knexRead: Knex,
    36→  @InjectConnection(KNEX_WRITE_CONNECTION) private readonly knexWrite: Knex,
    37→) {}
    38→```
    39→
    40→- Use `knexRead` for SELECT queries
    41→- Use `knexWrite` for INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE
    42→
    43→### Import Aliases (Required)
    44→
    45→```typescript
    46→import { SomeService } from '@modules/some/some.service';
    47→import { someUtil } from '@utils/some.util';
    48→import { SomeDto } from '@dto/some/some.dto';
    49→import { SOME_CONSTANT } from '@constants/some.constant';
    50→```
    51→
    52→**NEVER use relative imports** - ESLint will fail.
    53→
    54→### LogExecution Decorator (CRITICAL)
    55→
    56→**ALWAYS wrap important methods and 3rd party requests:**
    57→
    58→```typescript
    59→@LogExecution()
    60→async fetchFromThirdParty(params: Params): Promise<Result> {
    61→  // 3rd party API call
    62→}
    63→
    64→@LogExecution({
    65→  paramFormatter: (params) => ({ id: params.id }),
    66→  responseFormatter: () => '',
    67→})
    68→async complexOperation(params: ComplexParams): Promise<LargeData> {
    69→  // operation
    70→}
    71→```
    72→
    73→Use for:
    74→- All 3rd party API requests
    75→- Important business logic
    76→- Database operations that may be slow
    77→- Methods handling sensitive data
    78→
    79→### Logging
    80→
    81→```typescript
    82→import { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
    83→
    84→export class MyService {
    85→  private readonly logger = new Logger(MyService.name);
    86→
    87→  someMethod(): void {
    88→    this.logger.log('Message');
    89→    this.logger.error('Error', trace);
    90→  }
    91→}
    92→```
    93→
    94→## Workflow for Code Changes
    95→
    96→### 1. Inspect Existing Patterns
    97→
    98→**BEFORE creating any file**, inspect similar existing files:
    99→
   100→```bash
   101→# For services
   102→ls -la src/modules/*/services/*.service.ts
   103→
   104→# For controllers
   105→ls -la src/modules/rest/*/*.controller.ts
   106→
   107→# For DTOs
   108→ls -la src/dto/
   109→
   110→# For models
   111→ls -la src/modules/db/*/models/*.model.ts
   112→```
   113→
   114→Read 2-3 similar files to understand patterns.
   115→
   116→### 2. Create Component Following Pattern
   117→
   118→#### Creating a Service
   119→
   120→```typescript
   121→import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
   122→import { InjectConnection } from 'nest-knexjs';
   123→import { Knex } from 'knex';
   124→
   125→import { KNEX_READ_CONNECTION, KNEX_WRITE_CONNECTION } from '@constants/common.constant';
   126→import { LogExecution } from '@decorators/log-execution.decorator';
   127→
   128→@Injectable()
   129→export class MyService {
   130→  private readonly logger = new Logger(MyService.name);
   131→
   132→  constructor(
   133→    @InjectConnection(KNEX_READ_CONNECTION) private readonly knexRead: Knex,
   134→    @InjectConnection(KNEX_WRITE_CONNECTION) private readonly knexWrite: Knex,
   135→  ) {}
   136→
   137→  @LogExecution()
   138→  async getData(id: string): Promise<Data> {
   139→    return this.knexRead('table')
   140→      .where({ id })
   141→      .first();
   142→  }
   143→
   144→  @LogExecution()
   145→  async createData(data: CreateDto): Promise<void> {
   146→    await this.knexWrite('table').insert(data);
   147→  }
   148→}
   149→```
   150→
   151→#### Creating a Controller
   152→
   153→```typescript
   154→import { Controller, Get, Post, Body, Param, HttpStatus } from '@nestjs/common';
   155→import { ApiOperation, ApiResponse, ApiTags } from '@nestjs/swagger';
   156→
   157→import { MyService } from './my.service';
   158→import { CreateDto, ResponseDto } from '@dto/my/my.dto';
   159→
   160→@ApiTags('My Module')
   161→@Controller('my-endpoint')
   162→export class MyController {
   163→  constructor(private readonly myService: MyService) {}
   164→
   165→  @Get(':id')
   166→  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get data by ID' })
   167→  @ApiResponse({ status: HttpStatus.OK, description: 'Success' })
   168→  async getData(@Param('id') id: string): Promise<ResponseDto> {
   169→    return this.myService.getData(id);
   170→  }
   171→
   172→  @Post()
   173→  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Create new data' })
   174→  @ApiResponse({ status: HttpStatus.CREATED, description: 'Created' })
   175→  async createData(@Body() data: CreateDto): Promise<void> {
   176→    return this.myService.createData(data);
   177→  }
   178→}
   179→```
   180→
   181→#### Creating a Module
   182→
   183→```typescript
   184→import { Module } from '@nestjs/common';
   185→
   186→import { MyController } from './my.controller';
   187→import { MyService } from './my.service';
   188→import { OtherModule } from '@modules/other/other.module';
   189→
   190→@Module({
   191→  imports: [OtherModule],
   192→  controllers: [MyController],
   193→  providers: [MyService],
   194→  exports: [MyService],
   195→})
   196→export class MyModule {}
   197→```
   198→
   199→#### Creating a Guard
   200→
   201→```typescript
   202→import { Injectable, CanActivate, ExecutionContext, UnauthorizedException } from '@nestjs/common';
   203→import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
   204→
   205→@Injectable()
   206→export class MyGuard implements CanActivate {
   207→  canActivate(
   208→    context: ExecutionContext,
   209→  ): boolean | Promise<boolean> | Observable<boolean> {
   210→    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest();
   211→
   212→    if (!request.headers['authorization']) {
   213→      throw new UnauthorizedException('Missing authorization header');
   214→    }
   215→
   216→    return true;
   217→  }
   218→}
   219→```
   220→
   221→#### Creating a Decorator
   222→
   223→```typescript
   224→import { SetMetadata } from '@nestjs/common';
   225→
   226→export const METADATA_KEY = 'metadata_key';
   227→
   228→export const MyDecorator = (value: string): MethodDecorator =>
   229→  SetMetadata(METADATA_KEY, value);
   230→```
   231→
   232→#### Creating a Pipe
   233→
   234→```typescript
   235→import { PipeTransform, Injectable, BadRequestException } from '@nestjs/common';
   236→
   237→@Injectable()
   238→export class MyPipe implements PipeTransform {
   239→  transform(value: unknown): unknown {
   240→    if (!this.isValid(value)) {
   241→      throw new BadRequestException('Validation failed');
   242→    }
   243→    return value;
   244→  }
   245→
   246→  private isValid(value: unknown): boolean {
   247→    return true;
   248→  }
   249→}
   250→```
   251→
   252→### 3. Create DTOs
   253→
   254→Location: `/src/dto/{feature}/`
   255→
   256→```typescript
   257→import { IsString, IsNotEmpty, IsOptional, IsNumber } from 'class-validator';
   258→import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional } from '@nestjs/swagger';
   259→
   260→export class CreateDto {
   261→  @ApiProperty({ description: 'Name of the item' })
   262→  @IsString()
   263→  @IsNotEmpty()
   264→  name: string;
   265→
   266→  @ApiPropertyOptional({ description: 'Optional description' })
   267→  @IsString()
   268→  @IsOptional()
   269→  description?: string;
   270→
   271→  @ApiProperty({ description: 'Price in cents' })
   272→  @IsNumber()
   273→  price: number;
   274→}
   275→
   276→export class ResponseDto {
   277→  @ApiProperty()
   278→  id: string;
   279→
   280→  @ApiProperty()
   281→  name: string;
   282→
   283→  @ApiProperty()
   284→  createdAt: Date;
   285→}
   286→```
   287→
   288→### 4. Orchestrate Other Skills
   289→
   290→After writing code:
   291→
   292→**Always use code-quality-skill:**
   293→```
   294→After creating/updating code, automatically run:
   295→- npm run lint:fix
   296→- npm run format
   297→- npm run type-check
   298→- Fix any remaining issues
   299→```
   300→
   301→**Always use test-skill:**
   302→```
   303→After creating new components, write tests:
   304→- Unit tests in /src/unit-tests/
   305→- E2E tests in /test/
   306→- Ensure 95% coverage
   307→```
   308→
   309→**For debugging, use debug-agent:**
   310→```
   311→When encountering errors, test failures, or unexpected behavior
   312→```
   313→
   314→**For database work, use database-agent:**
   315→```
   316→When designing schemas, writing migrations, or optimizing queries
   317→```
   318→
   319→## Common Patterns
   320→
   321→### Dependency Injection
   322→
   323→```typescript
   324→// Inject services
   325→constructor(
   326→  private readonly myService: MyService,
   327→  private readonly otherService: OtherService,
   328→) {}
   329→
   330→// Inject with token
   331→constructor(
   332→  @Inject('CONFIG_OPTIONS') private readonly config: ConfigOptions,
   333→) {}
   334→
   335→// Inject Knex
   336→constructor(
   337→  @InjectConnection(KNEX_READ_CONNECTION) private readonly knexRead: Knex,
   338→  @InjectConnection(KNEX_WRITE_CONNECTION) private readonly knexWrite: Knex,
   339→) {}
   340→```
   341→
   342→### Async Operations
   343→
   344→```typescript
   345→@LogExecution()
   346→async fetchData(): Promise<Data[]> {
   347→  const [users, orders] = await Promise.all([
   348→    this.knexRead('users').select('*'),
   349→    this.knexRead('orders').select('*'),
   350→  ]);
   351→
   352→  return this.combineData(users, orders);
   353→}
   354→```
   355→
   356→### Error Handling
   357→
   358→```typescript
   359→import { HttpException, HttpStatus } from '@nestjs/common';
   360→
   361→@LogExecution()
   362→async getData(id: string): Promise<Data> {
   363→  const data = await this.knexRead('table').where({ id }).first();
   364→
   365→  if (!data) {
   366→    throw new HttpException('Data not found', HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND);
   367→  }
   368→
   369→  return data;
   370→}
   371→```
   372→
   373→### Environment Variables
   374→
   375→```typescript
   376→import { EnvConfigService } from '@modules/env-config/env-config.service';
   377→import { SOME_VAR } from '@constants/env-variables.constant';
   378→
   379→constructor(private readonly configService: EnvConfigService) {}
   380→
   381→someMethod(): void {
   382→  const value = this.configService.get<string>({ key: SOME_VAR });
   383→  const numValue = this.configService.get<number>({
   384→    key: SOME_VAR,
   385→    converter: Number
   386→  });
   387→}
   388→```
   389→
   390→### Caching
   391→
   392→```typescript
   393→import { CacheService } from '@modules/cache/cache.service';
   394→import { Namespaces } from '@interfaces/cache.interface';
   395→
   396→constructor(private readonly cacheService: CacheService) {}
   397→
   398→@LogExecution()
   399→async getWithCache(key: string): Promise<Data> {
   400→  const cached = await this.cacheService.get<Data>({
   401→    namespace: Namespaces.MY_NAMESPACE,
   402→    key,
   403→  });
   404→
   405→  if (cached) return cached;
   406→
   407→  const data = await this.fetchData(key);
   408→
   409→  await this.cacheService.set({
   410→    namespace: Namespaces.MY_NAMESPACE,
   411→    key,
   412→    value: data,
   413→    ttl: 3600,
   414→  });
   415→
   416→  return data;
   417→}
   418→```
   419→
   420→## Performance Optimization
   421→
   422→### Database Query Optimization
   423→
   424→```typescript
   425→// Use select to limit fields
   426→const data = await this.knexRead('table')
   427→  .select('id', 'name', 'email')
   428→  .where({ active: true });
   429→
   430→// Use pagination
   431→const data = await this.knexRead('table')
   432→  .limit(limit)
   433→  .offset(offset);
   434→
   435→// Use joins efficiently
   436→const data = await this.knexRead('users as u')
   437→  .join('orders as o', 'u.id', 'o.user_id')
   438→  .select('u.*', 'o.total');
   439→```
   440→
   441→### Async Optimization
   442→
   443→```typescript
   444→// Parallel execution
   445→const [data1, data2, data3] = await Promise.all([
   446→  this.service1.getData(),
   447→  this.service2.getData(),
   448→  this.service3.getData(),
   449→]);
   450→
   451→// Sequential when dependencies exist
   452→const user = await this.getUser(id);
   453→const orders = await this.getOrders(user.id);
   454→```
   455→
   456→### Memory Management
   457→
   458→```typescript
   459→// Stream large datasets
   460→async *streamData(): AsyncGenerator<Data> {
   461→  const stream = this.knexRead('table').stream();
   462→
   463→  for await (const row of stream) {
   464→    yield this.transform(row);
   465→  }
   466→}
   467→```
   468→
   469→## TypeScript Best Practices
   470→
   471→### Strict Types
   472→
   473→```typescript
   474→// Explicit return types
   475→async getData(id: string): Promise<Data | null> {
   476→  return this.knexRead('table').where({ id }).first();
   477→}
   478→
   479→// Generic types
   480→async getMany<T>(ids: string[]): Promise<T[]> {
   481→  return this.knexRead('table').whereIn('id', ids);
   482→}
   483→
   484→// Union types
   485→type Status = 'active' | 'inactive' | 'pending';
   486→
   487→async getByStatus(status: Status): Promise<Data[]> {
   488→  return this.knexRead('table').where({ status });
   489→}
   490→```
   491→
   492→### Interface vs Type
   493→
   494→```typescript
   495→// Use interface for objects
   496→interface User {
   497→  id: string;
   498→  name: string;
   499→  email: string;
   500→}
   501→
   502→// Use type for unions/intersections
   503→type UserStatus = 'active' | 'inactive';
   504→type UserWithStatus = User & { status: UserStatus };
   505→```
   506→
   507→## Debugging Tips
   508→
   509→### Common Issues
   510→
   511→**Circular Dependency:**
   512→```typescript
   513→// Use forwardRef
   514→@Module({
   515→  imports: [forwardRef(() => OtherModule)],
   516→})
   517→```
   518→
   519→**Injection Token Not Found:**
   520→```typescript
   521→// Ensure provider is in module
   522→@Module({
   523→  providers: [MyService],
   524→  exports: [MyService],
   525→})
   526→```
   527→
   528→**Database Connection Issues:**
   529→```typescript
   530→// Check KNEX_READ_CONNECTION and KNEX_WRITE_CONNECTION
   531→// Verify connection in module imports
   532→```
   533→
   534→## File Organization
   535→
   536→```
   537→src/modules/{feature}/
   538→├── {feature}.module.ts
   539→├── {feature}.controller.ts
   540→├── {feature}.service.ts
   541→├── dto/                    # Feature-specific DTOs (if not in /src/dto/)
   542→├── guards/                 # Feature-specific guards
   543→└── decorators/             # Feature-specific decorators
   544→
   545→src/dto/{feature}/
   546→├── create-{feature}.dto.ts
   547→├── update-{feature}.dto.ts
   548→└── response-{feature}.dto.ts
   549→
   550→src/unit-tests/
   551→└── {feature}.service.unit-spec.ts
   552→
   553→test/
   554→└── {feature}.e2e-spec.ts
   555→```
   556→
   557→## Success Checklist
   558→
   559→Before considering code complete:
   560→
   561→- ✅ Inspected existing similar files for patterns
   562→- ✅ NO COMMENTS or EXPLANATIONS in code
   563→- ✅ Used path aliases (no relative imports)
   564→- ✅ Database read/write pattern followed
   565→- ✅ @LogExecution on important methods
   566→- ✅ Logger from @nestjs/common used
   567→- ✅ Explicit return types on all methods
   568→- ✅ DTOs with validation decorators
   569→- ✅ Swagger decorators on controllers
   570→- ✅ Ran code-quality-skill (lint, format, type-check pass)
   571→- ✅ Ran test-skill (tests written, 95% coverage)
   572→- ✅ All imports use aliases
   573→- ✅ Environment variables use EnvConfigService
   574→

     1→---
     2→name: fix-eslint-workflow
     3→description: Full workflow for fixing ESLint issues in this NestJS/TypeScript repo. Use when a Jira ticket (RECO-XXX) describes ESLint rule violations to fix, especially: object destructuring with explicitly named params in function signatures, import path aliases, explicit return types, or any ESLint/TypeScript code quality fix that requires updating dependent files, imports, and tests. Triggers on: "fix eslint issues for RECO-XXX", "fix eslint rules in file X", "run eslint checks and fix", "enforce named params rule".
     4→---
     5→
     6→# Fix ESLint Workflow
     7→
     8→End-to-end workflow: Jira ticket → branch → eslint audit → fix violations → fix dependents → fix tests → verify.
     9→
    10→## Workflow
    11→
    12→### 1. Check Jira ticket
    13→
    14→Fetch the ticket to understand which files and ESLint rules are in scope:
    15→
    16→```bash
    17→grep ATLASSIAN_CLOUD_ID .env | cut -d'=' -f2
    18→```
    19→
    20→Use `getJiraIssue` with the cloud ID and ticket key. Identify:
    21→
    22→- Which files need fixing
    23→- Which ESLint rule is being enforced
    24→
    25→### 2. Create branch from current branch
    26→
    27→```bash
    28→git checkout -b RECO-XXX-descriptive-name
    29→```
    30→
    31→Branch from current branch (NOT main). Branch name: `RECO-XXX-kebab-case-summary`.
    32→
    33→### 3. Run ESLint on target files
    34→
    35→```bash
    36→npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0 path/to/file.ts
    37→```
    38→
    39→Capture the full error/warning list before making any changes.
    40→
    41→### 4. Fix ESLint violations
    42→
    43→Plan your fixes before coding:
    44→
    45→1. Identify the ESLint violation(s) in the specified file(s)
    46→2. Use Grep to find ALL callers and imports of affected functions
    47→3. Apply the fix using named object params pattern (not shortcuts)
    48→4. Update ALL test files — mock expectations AND assertions
    49→5. Run `npm run test:coverage` and `npm run code-quality-check` — do not stop until both pass
    50→6. Show a summary of all files changed
    51→
    52→Apply fixes in this priority order:
    53→
    54→#### a. Auto-fix first
    55→
    56→```bash
    57→npm run lint:fix
    58→```
    59→
    60→#### b. Fix remaining manually
    61→
    62→See [references/eslint-patterns.md](references/eslint-patterns.md) for common patterns specific to this repo.
    63→
    64→**Most common manual fixes:**
    65→
    66→**Named object params (most common RECO fix)**
    67→
    68→```typescript
    69→// ❌ Positional params
    70→function myFn(id: string, name: string): void {}
    71→
    72→// ✅ Object with named params
    73→function myFn({ id, name }: { id: string; name: string }): void {}
    74→```
    75→
    76→**Path aliases (no relative imports)**
    77→
    78→```typescript
    79→// ❌
    80→import { Foo } from '../../modules/foo/foo.service';
    81→// ✅
    82→import { Foo } from '@modules/foo/foo.service';
    83→```
    84→
    85→**Explicit return types**
    86→
    87→```typescript
    88→// ❌
    89→async function getData() {
    90→	return data;
    91→}
    92→// ✅
    93→async function getData(): Promise<Data> {
    94→	return data;
    95→}
    96→```
    97→
    98→### 5. Fix ALL callers of changed functions (CRITICAL)
    99→
   100→**This step is mandatory.** After changing any function/method signature, you MUST find and update every call site across the entire project — not just the file you edited.
   101→
   102→Find all callers:
   103→
   104→```bash
   105→grep -r "functionName" src/ test/ --include="*.ts" -l
   106→```
   107→
   108→Or use the Grep tool to search for the function name.
   109→
   110→Update **every** call site to use the new object param syntax:
   111→
   112→```typescript
   113→// ❌ Old call
   114→validate(configRecord);
   115→service.myFn(id, name);
   116→
   117→// ✅ New named call
   118→validate({ envConfig: configRecord });
   119→service.myFn({ id, name });
   120→```
   121→
   122→**WARNING: Do NOT use rest-spread `{ ...param }` as a shortcut.** It avoids updating callers but changes the semantic (creates a copy) and produces confusing code. Always use explicit named params: `{ envConfig }`, `{ styleId, colorId }`, etc.
   123→
   124→Check these locations in order:
   125→
   126→1. Same module's controller/service
   127→2. Other modules importing this service
   128→3. CLI commands in `src/modules/cli/`
   129→4. Scripts in `src/scripts/`
   130→5. Test files (`src/unit-tests/`, `test/`)
   131→6. RFC/documentation files (revert accidental changes to unrelated `validate` from `class-validator`)
   132→
   133→### 6. Fix tests
   134→
   135→Unit tests: `src/unit-tests/*.unit-spec.ts`
   136→E2E tests: `test/*.e2e-spec.ts`
   137→
   138→Update mock calls AND assertions to match new signatures:
   139→
   140→```typescript
   141→// ❌
   142→service.myFn(id, name);
   143→expect(service.myFn).toHaveBeenCalledWith(id, name);
   144→
   145→// ✅
   146→service.myFn({ id, name });
   147→expect(service.myFn).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ id: id, name: name });
   148→```
   149→
   150→Run full coverage to confirm — do not stop until all checks pass:
   151→
   152→```bash
   153→npm run test:coverage
   154→```
   155→
   156→### 7. Verify ESLint on all changed files
   157→
   158→```bash
   159→npm run lint
   160→npm run type-check
   161→```
   162→
   163→All checks must pass before done.
   164→
   165→## Common Mistakes
   166→
   167→| Mistake                                       | Fix                                           |
   168→| --------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- |
   169→| Forgetting to update call sites               | Grep for all usages after changing signatures |
   170→| Missing test updates                          | Run `npm run test:unit` after fixing code     |
   171→| Using relative imports                        | Use `@modules/`, `@utils/`, `@dto/` etc.      |
   172→| Breaking return types                         | Add explicit `: Promise<T>` or `: void`       |
   173→| Branching from main instead of current branch | `git checkout -b` from current branch         |
   174→
   175→## Resources
   176→
   177→- **[references/eslint-patterns.md](references/eslint-patterns.md)**: Repo-specific ESLint rule patterns and fix examples
   178→

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

     1→---
     2→name: create-pr
     3→description: Create pull requests with automated validation against Jira tasks. Use when user requests to create a PR, make a pull request, or open a pull request. Validates committed changes against Jira task description, identifies missing work, gets user confirmation, and creates draft PR with fulfilled template.
     4→---
     5→
     6→# Create PR
     7→
     8→Automated pull request creation workflow with Jira task validation.
     9→
    10→## Workflow
    11→
    12→Follow these steps sequentially:
    13→
    14→### 1. Get Atlassian Cloud ID
    15→
    16→Read cloud ID from environment file:
    17→
    18→```bash
    19→grep ATLASSIAN_CLOUD_ID .env | cut -d'=' -f2
    20→```
    21→
    22→Store for use in Jira API calls.
    23→
    24→### 2. Validate Branch State
    25→
    26→Check current branch and commit status:
    27→
    28→```bash
    29→git branch --show-current
    30→git log origin/main..HEAD --oneline
    31→```
    32→
    33→**Exit conditions:**
    34→- Current branch is `main` or `master` → Respond: "No changes"
    35→- No commits ahead of main → Respond: "No changes"
    36→
    37→### 3. Extract Jira Task ID
    38→
    39→Parse task ID from branch name using pattern `RECO-\d+`:
    40→
    41→**Examples:**
    42→- `RECO-349-task-naming` → Task ID: `RECO-349`
    43→- `RECO-1234-fix-bug` → Task ID: `RECO-1234`
    44→
    45→If no match found → Ask user: "Cannot extract task ID from branch name. Provide task ID or continue without validation?"
    46→
    47→### 4. Fetch Jira Task
    48→
    49→Use Atlassian MCP tool with cloud ID from step 1:
    50→
    51→```typescript
    52→mcp__plugin_atlassian_atlassian__getJiraIssue({
    53→  cloudId: CLOUD_ID_FROM_STEP_1,
    54→  issueIdOrKey: "RECO-349"
    55→})
    56→```
    57→
    58→Extract:
    59→- Task title
    60→- Task description
    61→- Acceptance criteria
    62→- Subtasks (if any)
    63→
    64→### 5. Analyze Committed Changes
    65→
    66→Review all commits in current branch:
    67→
    68→```bash
    69→git log origin/main..HEAD --stat
    70→git diff origin/main..HEAD
    71→```
    72→
    73→Compare changes against:
    74→- Task description requirements
    75→- Acceptance criteria
    76→
    77→### 6. Identify Gaps
    78→
    79→Create list of missing items:
    80→- Uncompleted requirements from task description
    81→- Missing acceptance criteria implementations
    82→- Missing tests (if specified)
    83→
    84→**Format:**
    85→```
    86→Missing items:
    87→- [ ] Item 1 description
    88→- [ ] Item 2 description
    89→- [ ] Item 3 description
    90→```
    91→
    92→If no gaps found → State: "All task requirements appear to be implemented."
    93→
    94→### 7. Request Confirmation
    95→
    96→Present findings and ask for confirmation:
    97→
    98→```
    99→Summary:
   100→- Branch: [branch-name]
   101→- Task: [TASK-ID] - [task title]
   102→- Commits: [count] commits
   103→
   104→[Missing items list or "All requirements met"]
   105→
   106→Create draft PR with current state?
   107→```
   108→
   109→Wait for user confirmation. If declined → Stop.
   110→
   111→### 8. Fetch PR Template
   112→
   113→Read repository PR template:
   114→
   115→```bash
   116→cat .github/PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md
   117→```
   118→
   119→If not found → Check for:
   120→- `.github/pull_request_template.md`
   121→- `docs/pull_request_template.md`
   122→- `PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md`
   123→
   124→### 9. Create Draft PR
   125→
   126→Fill PR template with:
   127→- **Title**: `[TASK-ID]: [task title from Jira]`
   128→- **Description**: Task description from Jira
   129→- **Changes**: Summary of committed changes
   130→
   131→Create draft PR:
   132→
   133→```bash
   134→gh pr create \
   135→  --draft \
   136→  --title "[TASK-ID]: [title]" \
   137→  --body "$(cat <<'EOF'
   138→[Filled template content]
   139→EOF
   140→)"
   141→```
   142→
   143→Respond with PR URL and summary.
   144→
   145→## Notes
   146→
   147→- Always create as **draft** PR
   148→- Main branch detection: `main` or `master`
   149→- Task ID pattern: `RECO-\d+` (project-specific, adjust if needed)
   150→- Use existing tools: `gh` CLI, Atlassian MCP, Git
   151→- Validation is advisory, not blocking - user decides whether to proceed

Use when visualizing system architecture, data flows, class relationships, API interactions, database schemas, or any technical process.      5→
     6→# Diagram Skill
     7→
     8→Create technical diagrams using ASCII art for documentation and visualization.
     9→
    10→## When to Use
    11→
    12→Trigger when:
    13→- Visualizing feature flows or business logic
    14→- Documenting API interactions
    15→- Showing database relationships
    16→- Illustrating class hierarchies
    17→- Mapping system architecture
    18→- Explaining request/response cycles
    19→- Documenting data flows

    21→## Diagram Types
    22→
    23→### Sequence Diagrams (ASCII Art)
    24→
    25→Show interactions between components over time using boxes, lines, and arrows.
    26→
    27→**Pattern:**
    28→
    29→```
    30→┌─────────┐           ┌────────────┐         ┌─────────┐         ┌──────────┐
    31→│ Client  │           │ Controller │         │ Service │         │ Database │
    32→└────┬────┘           └─────┬──────┘         └────┬────┘         └────┬─────┘
    33→     │                      │                     │                   │
    34→     │ POST /api/endpoint   │                     │                   │
    35→     │─────────────────────>│                     │                   │
    36→     │                      │                     │                   │
    37→     │                      │  processData(dto)   │                   │
    38→     │                      │────────────────────>│                   │
    39→     │                      │                     │                   │
    40→     │                      │                     │  INSERT INTO      │
    41→     │                      │                     │──────────────────>│
    42→     │                      │                     │                   │
    43→     │                      │                     │      Result       │
    44→     │                      │                     │<──────────────────│
    45→     │                      │                     │                   │
    46→     │                      │   ProcessedData     │                   │
    47→     │                      │<────────────────────│                   │
    48→     │                      │                     │                   │
    49→     │    201 Created       │                     │                   │
    50→     │<─────────────────────│                     │                   │
    51→     │                      │                     │                   │
    52→```
    53→
    54→**Elements:**
    55→- `┌─┐ └─┘` - Component boxes
    56→- `─────>` - Request arrow (solid)

   272→
   273→## Best Practices
   274→
   275→### Clear Component Names
   276→
   277→Use full, descriptive names in boxes:
   278→```
   279→┌────────────────────────┐
   280→│ RelatedProductsController │
   281→└────────────────────────┘
   282→```
   283→
   284→### Show Activation/Deactivation
   285→
   286→Use indentation or annotations:
   287→```
   288→Controller
   289→  │ [ACTIVE]
   290→  │──> Service
   291→  │      [ACTIVE]
   292→  │      └──> Database
   293→  │            [ACTIVE]
   294→  │            Result
   295→  │      <────
   296→  │      [INACTIVE]
   297→  │ <────
   298→  │ [INACTIVE]
   299→```
   300→
   301→### Add Notes
   302→
   303→Use text annotations:
   304→```
   305→     │  get(key)        │
   306→     │─────────────────>│
   307→     │                  │  // Checks Redis cache
   308→     │     null         │  // with 1-hour TTL
   309→     │<─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─│
   310→```
   311→
   312→### Error Paths
   313→
   314→Show alternative flows clearly:
   315→```
   316→    ┌──────────────┐
   317→    │ Validate DTO │
   318→    └──────┬───────┘
   319→           │
   320→    ┌──────▼─────────┐
   321→    │  Valid?        │
   322→    └──┬────────────┬┘
   323→       │ Yes        │ No
   324→       │            │
   325→       ▼            ▼
   326→   [Success]    [400 Error]
   327→    Path         Path
   328→```
   329→
   330→### Parallel Operations
   331→
   332→Show side-by-side:
   333→```
   334→Service
   335→  │
   336→  ├─────────────────┬─────────────────┬─────────────────┐
   337→  │                 │                 │                 │
   338→  ▼                 ▼                 ▼                 ▼
   339→┌────────┐     ┌────────┐     ┌────────┐     ┌────────┐
   340→│ Fetch  │     │ Fetch  │     │ Fetch  │     │ Fetch  │
   341→│ Users  │     │ Orders │     │Products│     │ Stats  │
   342→└───┬────┘     └───┬────┘     └───┬────┘     └───┬────┘
   343→    │              │              │              │
   344→    └──────────────┴──────────────┴──────────────┘
   345→                   │
   346→                   ▼
   347→            Combine Results
   348→```
   349→
   350→## Common NestJS Patterns
   351→
   352→### CRUD Operation with Guards
   353→
   354→```
   355→┌─────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌────────────┐  ┌────────────────┐  ┌─────────┐  ┌───────┐  ┌──────────┐
   356→│ Client  │  │AuthGuard │  │ Controller │  │ValidationPipe  │  │ Service │  │ Model │  │ Knex(W)  │
   357→└────┬────┘  └────┬─────┘  └─────┬──────┘  └───────┬────────┘  └────┬────┘  └───┬───┘  └────┬─────┘
   358→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │           │
   359→     │ POST /api  │               │                 │                 │           │           │
   360→     │───────────>│               │                 │                 │           │           │
   361→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │           │
   362→     │            │ Verify JWT    │                 │                 │           │           │
   363→     │            │───────────────>│                 │                 │           │           │
   364→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │           │
   365→     │            │    Authorized │                 │                 │           │           │
   366→     │            │<───────────────│                 │                 │           │           │
   367→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │           │
   368→     │            │               │  Validate DTO   │                 │           │           │
   369→     │            │               │────────────────>│                 │           │           │
   370→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │           │
   371→     │            │               │    Valid DTO    │                 │           │           │
   372→     │            │               │<────────────────│                 │           │           │
   373→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │           │
   374→     │            │               │      create(dto)│                 │           │           │
   375→     │            │               │────────────────────────────────────>           │           │
   376→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │           │
   377→     │            │               │                 │                 │create(data)           │
   378→     │            │               │                 │                 │──────────>│           │
   379→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │           │
   380→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │INSERT INTO│
   381→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │──────────>│
   382→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │           │
   383→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │  Result   │
   384→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │<──────────│
   385→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │           │
   386→     │            │               │                 │                 │   Entity  │           │
   387→     │            │               │                 │                 │<──────────│           │
   388→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │           │
   389→     │            │               │                 │      EntityDto  │           │           │
   390→     │            │               │<────────────────────────────────────           │           │
   391→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │           │
   392→     │ 201 Created│               │                 │                 │           │           │
   393→     │<───────────────────────────│                 │                 │           │           │
   394→     │            │               │                 │                 │           │           │
   395→```
   396→
   397→### Database Read/Write Pattern
   398→
   399→```
   400→API Request
   401→    │
   402→    ▼
   403→┌───────────────┐
   404→│ Determine Op  │
   405→└───────┬───────┘
   406→        │
   407→        │
   408→    ┌───▼────┐
   409→    │  Type? │
   410→    └───┬────┘
   411→        │
   412→  ┌─────┴─────┐
   413→  │           │
   414→Read          Write
   415→  │           │
   416→  ▼           ▼
   417→┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐
   418→│Knex Read │ │Knex Write│
   419→│Connection│ │Connection│
   420→└────┬─────┘ └────┬─────┘
   421→     │            │
   422→     │            ▼
   423→     │      ┌────────────┐
   424→     │      │Transaction │
   425→     │      └────┬───────┘
   426→     │           │
   427→     └─────┬─────┘
   428→           │
   429→           ▼
   430→      ┌─────────┐
   431→      │Response │
   432→      └─────────┘
   433→```
   434→
   435→### Caching Strategy
   436→
   437→```
   438→┌─────────┐   ┌─────────┐   ┌──────────┐   ┌──────────┐
   439→│ Client  │   │ Service │   │  Cache   │   │ Database │
   440→└────┬────┘   └────┬────┘   └────┬─────┘   └────┬─────┘
   441→     │             │              │              │
   442→     │ getData(id) │              │              │
   443→     │────────────>│              │              │
   444→     │             │              │              │
   445→     │             │ get(key)     │              │
   446→     │             │─────────────>│              │
   447→     │             │              │              │
   448→     │             │   Hit/Miss?  │              │
   449→     │             │              │              │
   450→     │             ├──────────────┤              │
   451→     │             │              │              │
   452→     │         If Hit:            │              │
   453→     │             │ Data         │              │
   454→     │             │<─────────────│              │
   455→     │             │              │              │
   456→     │      Data   │              │              │
   457→     │<────────────│              │              │
   458→     │             │              │              │
   459→     │         If Miss:           │              │
   460→     │             │              │              │
   461→     │             │        Query database       │
   462→     │             │───────────────────────────> │
   463→     │             │              │              │
   464→     │             │              │    Data      │
   465→     │             │<─────────────────────────── │
   466→     │             │              │              │
   467→     │             │ set(key,data)│              │
   468→     │             │─────────────>│              │
   469→     │             │              │              │
   470→     │             │      OK      │              │
   471→     │             │<─────────────│              │
   472→     │             │              │              │
   473→     │      Data   │              │              │
   474→     │<────────────│              │              │
   475→     │             │              │              │
   476→```
   477→
   478→## Output Format
   479→
   480→Output diagrams as code blocks:
   481→
   482→```
   483→[ASCII art diagram here]
   484→```
   485→
   486→No special rendering needed - pure text displays everywhere.

   488→## Success Checklist
   489→

   491→- ✅ Clear component boxes with full names
   492→- ✅ Logical flow (top-to-bottom or left-to-right)

   494→- ✅ Labels on all arrows/connections
   495→- ✅ Consistent spacing and formatting
   496→- ✅ Error paths shown (if applicable)
   497→- ✅ Notes for complex operations
   498→- ✅ Readable in any text editor/viewer
   499→- ✅ Uses box-drawing characters properly

     1→---
     2→name: documentation-skill
     3→description: Comprehensive technical documentation creation and maintenance in the docs/ directory. Use when asked to describe features, explain business logic, document tools, or create technical specifications. Automatically creates markdown documentation with code examples, ASCII sequence diagrams (using diagram-skill), architecture diagrams, and comprehensive explanations. Triggers on requests like "document this feature", "explain how X works", "create docs for", or when new features/logic need documentation.
     4→---
     5→
     6→# Documentation Skill
     7→
     8→Create and maintain comprehensive technical documentation in `/docs/` with code examples and ASCII art diagrams.
     9→
    10→## When to Use
    11→
    12→Trigger when:
    13→- User asks to document features or logic
    14→- New features need documentation
    15→- Existing docs need updates
    16→- Explaining system architecture
    17→- Describing API endpoints
    18→- Documenting database schemas
    19→- Tool/utility documentation needed
    20→- Business logic needs explanation
    21→
    22→## Documentation Location
    23→
    24→All documentation lives in:
    25→```
    26→docs/
    27→├── features/           # Feature documentation
    28→├── architecture/       # System architecture
    29→├── api/               # API documentation
    30→├── database/          # Database schemas
    31→├── tools/             # CLI tools & utilities
    32→└── guides/            # How-to guides
    33→```
    34→
    35→## Documentation Structure
    36→
    37→### Standard Template
    38→
    39→Every documentation file should include:
    40→
    41→1. **Title & Overview**
    42→2. **Use Cases**
    43→3. **Architecture** (with diagrams)
    44→4. **Data Flow** (with ASCII sequence diagrams for features/business logic)
    45→5. **Code Examples**
    46→6. **API Reference** (if applicable)
    47→7. **Configuration** (if applicable)
    48→8. **Testing**
    49→9. **Troubleshooting**
    50→
    51→## Workflow
    52→
    53→### 1. Understand What to Document
    54→
    55→Analyze:
    56→- Feature/module code
    57→- Related services
    58→- Database models
    59→- API endpoints
    60→- Configuration
    61→
    62→### 2. Determine Documentation Type
    63→
    64→**Feature Documentation:**
    65→- Location: `docs/features/{feature-name}.md`
    66→- **MUST include ASCII sequence diagram** showing flow
    67→- Includes use cases, architecture, examples
    68→
    69→**Architecture Documentation:**
    70→- Location: `docs/architecture/{component}.md`
    71→- System design, patterns, decisions
    72→- Class diagrams, ER diagrams
    73→
    74→**API Documentation:**
    75→- Location: `docs/api/{endpoint}.md`
    76→- Endpoint details, request/response examples
    77→- Sequence diagrams for complex flows
    78→
    79→**Database Documentation:**
    80→- Location: `docs/database/{table}.md`
    81→- Schema, relationships, indexes
    82→- ER diagrams
    83→
    84→### 3. Use diagram-skill for Diagrams
    85→
    86→For **every feature or business logic** documentation:
    87→
    88→**REQUIRED: ASCII Sequence Diagram**
    89→- Shows complete request/response flow
    90→- Includes all components (Controller, Service, Model, DB, Cache, 3rd party APIs)
    91→- Shows error paths
    92→- Uses diagram-skill to generate ASCII art
    93→
    94→**Optional: Additional Diagrams**
    95→- Class diagrams for complex relationships
    96→- ER diagrams for database docs
    97→- Flowcharts for decision trees
    98→
    99→### 4. Write Documentation
   100→
   101→Follow template pattern below.
   102→
   103→### 5. Save to Appropriate Directory
   104→
   105→Create file in correct location under `/docs/`.
   106→
   107→## Template: Feature Documentation
   108→
   109→```markdown
   110→# Feature Name
   111→
   112→## Overview
   113→
   114→Brief description of what this feature does and why it exists.
   115→
   116→## Use Cases
   117→
   118→- **Use Case 1**: Description
   119→- **Use Case 2**: Description
   120→- **Use Case 3**: Description
   121→
   122→## Architecture
   123→
   124→### Components
   125→
   126→- **Controller**: `FeatureController` - Handles HTTP requests
   127→- **Service**: `FeatureService` - Business logic
   128→- **Model**: `FeatureModel` - Database operations
   129→- **DTO**: `FeatureCreateDto`, `FeatureDto` - Data validation
   130→
   131→### Dependencies
   132→
   133→- `CacheService` - Redis caching
   134→- `AlgoliaService` - Search functionality
   135→- `MmsService` - External API integration
   136→

   138→
   139→### Happy Path
   140→

   143→\`\`\`
   144→┌─────────┐   ┌─────────────────┐   ┌─────────────────┐   ┌──────────────┐   ┌────────────┐   ┌─────────────────┐
   145→│ Client  │   │FeatureController│   │ FeatureService  │   │ CacheService │   │ Knex(Read) │   │ ExternalAPI     │
   146→└────┬────┘   └────────┬────────┘   └────────┬────────┘   └──────┬───────┘   └─────┬──────┘   └────────┬────────┘
   147→     │                 │                      │                   │                 │                   │
   148→     │ POST /api/      │                      │                   │                 │                   │

   161→     │                 │                      │                   │                 │                   │

   167→     │                 │                      │                   │                 │                   │
   168→     │                 │                      │  fetchAdditional(data)              │                   │

   234→
   235→## Code Examples
   236→
   237→### Basic Usage
   238→
   239→\`\`\`typescript
   240→// Example 1: Create new feature
   241→const dto: CreateFeatureDto = {
   242→  name: 'Example',
   243→  value: 123,
   244→};
   245→
   246→const result = await featureService.create(dto);
   247→console.log(result); // { id: 'uuid', name: 'Example', ... }
   248→\`\`\`
   249→
   250→### With Caching
   251→
   252→\`\`\`typescript
   253→// Example 2: Using cache
   254→const cached = await featureService.getWithCache(id);
   255→\`\`\`
   256→
   257→### Error Handling
   258→
   259→\`\`\`typescript
   260→// Example 3: Handling errors
   261→try {
   262→  await featureService.create(dto);
   263→} catch (error) {
   264→  if (error instanceof ValidationError) {
   265→    // Handle validation
   266→  } else if (error instanceof NotFoundError) {
   267→    // Handle not found
   268→  }
   269→}
   270→\`\`\`
   271→
   272→## API Reference
   273→
   274→### POST /api/feature
   275→
   276→Create new feature.
   277→
   278→**Request:**
   279→
   280→\`\`\`typescript
   281→{
   282→  "name": "string",
   283→  "value": number,
   284→  "options": {
   285→    "flag": boolean
   286→  }
   287→}
   288→\`\`\`
   289→
   290→**Response:**
   291→
   292→\`\`\`typescript
   293→{
   294→  "id": "uuid",
   295→  "name": "string",
   296→  "value": number,
   297→  "created_at": "2025-01-18T10:00:00Z"
   298→}
   299→\`\`\`
   300→
   301→**Status Codes:**
   302→- `201` - Created successfully
   303→- `400` - Validation error
   304→- `500` - Server error
   305→
   306→### GET /api/feature/:id
   307→
   308→Retrieve feature by ID.
   309→
   310→**Parameters:**
   311→- `id` (path) - Feature UUID
   312→
   313→**Response:**
   314→
   315→\`\`\`typescript
   316→{
   317→  "id": "uuid",
   318→  "name": "string",
   319→  "value": number,
   320→  "created_at": "2025-01-18T10:00:00Z",
   321→  "updated_at": "2025-01-18T12:00:00Z"
   322→}
   323→\`\`\`
   324→
   325→**Status Codes:**
   326→- `200` - Success
   327→- `404` - Not found
   328→
   329→## Configuration
   330→
   331→### Environment Variables
   332→
   333→\`\`\`bash
   334→# Required environment variables
   335→FEATURE_CACHE_TTL=3600  # Cache duration in seconds
   336→FEATURE_API_KEY=xxx     # External API key
   337→\`\`\`
   338→
   339→### Module Configuration
   340→
   341→\`\`\`typescript

   343→  imports: [
   344→    CacheModule,
   345→    ExternalApiModule,
   346→  ],
   347→  controllers: [FeatureController],
   348→  providers: [FeatureService],
   349→  exports: [FeatureService],
   350→})
   351→export class FeatureModule {}
   352→\`\`\`
   353→
   354→## Database Schema
   355→
   356→### Table: features
   357→
   358→\`\`\`sql
   359→CREATE TABLE features (
   360→  _id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
   361→  name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
   362→  value INTEGER NOT NULL,
   363→  created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW(),
   364→  updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW(),
   365→  deleted_at TIMESTAMP NULL,
   366→  INDEX idx_name_deleted (name, deleted_at),
   367→  UNIQUE KEY uk_name (name) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL
   368→);
   369→\`\`\`
   370→
   371→[Use diagram-skill for ER diagram]
   372→
   373→\`\`\`
   374→┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
   375→│            features                 │
   376→├─────────────────────────────────────┤
   377→│ PK  _id (UUID)                      │

   380→│     created_at (TIMESTAMP)          │
   381→│     updated_at (TIMESTAMP)          │
   382→│     deleted_at (TIMESTAMP)          │
   383→└─────────────┬───────────────────────┘
   384→              │ 1
   385→              │
   386→              │ has
   387→              │
   388→              │ *
   389→              ▼
   390→┌──────────────────────────┐

   394→│ FK feature_id            │
   395→│    relation_type         │

   397→└──────────────────────────┘

   400→  PK = Primary Key

   405→\`\`\`
   406→
   407→## Testing
   408→
   409→### Unit Tests
   410→
   411→Located: `src/unit-tests/feature.service.unit-spec.ts`
   412→
   413→\`\`\`typescript
   414→describe('FeatureService', () => {
   415→  it('should create feature', async () => {
   416→    const dto = { name: 'test', value: 123 };
   417→    const result = await service.create(dto);
   418→    expect(result.name).toBe('test');
   419→  });
   420→});
   421→\`\`\`
   422→
   423→### E2E Tests
   424→
   425→Located: `test/feature.e2e-spec.ts`
   426→
   427→\`\`\`typescript

   436→\`\`\`
   437→
   438→## Performance Considerations
   439→
   440→- **Caching**: All reads cached for 1 hour
   441→- **Database**: Uses read replica for SELECT queries
   442→- **Batch Operations**: Processes in batches of 100
   443→- **Indexes**: Optimized for name lookups
   444→
   445→## Troubleshooting
   446→
   447→### Common Issues
   448→
   449→**Issue: Cache miss on every request**
   450→- **Cause**: Cache key not constructed correctly
   451→- **Solution**: Verify namespace and key format
   452→
   453→**Issue: Slow queries**
   454→- **Cause**: Missing index on frequently queried column
   455→- **Solution**: Add index in migration
   456→
   457→**Issue: Validation errors**
   458→- **Cause**: DTO validation rules not met
   459→- **Solution**: Check class-validator decorators
   460→
   461→## Related Documentation
   462→
   463→- [Database Schema](../database/features.md)
   464→- [Caching Strategy](../architecture/caching.md)
   465→- [API Guidelines](../guides/api-guidelines.md)
   466→```

   599→// Controller
   600→@Get(':id')
   601→@ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get by ID' })
   602→async getById(@Param('id') id: string): Promise<ResponseDto> {
   603→  return this.service.findById(id);
   604→}
   605→
   606→// Service
   607→async findById(id: string): Promise<ResponseDto> {
   608→  const result = await this.model.findById({ _id: id });
   609→  if (!result) {
   610→    throw new NotFoundException('Not found');
   611→  }
   612→  return result;
   613→}

   620→# Table Name

   624→Description of table purpose and data it stores.

   626→## Schema

   628→\`\`\`sql

   646→\`\`\`
   647→┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
   648→│            table_name               │
   649→├─────────────────────────────────────┤

   651→│ UK  entity_id (BIGINT)              │

   671→Legend:

```
     1→# ESLint Patterns - recommendations-service
     2→
     3→## Project Config
     4→
     5→- ESLint v9+ flat config (`eslint.config.mjs`)
     6→- TypeScript strict mode + `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, `exactOptionalPropertyTypes`
     7→- Prettier integrated
     8→
     9→IMPORTANT: read eslint config file before working with code to understand what is required
    10→
    11→## Rule: Named Object Params
    12→
    13→**Rule trigger:** Functions receiving multiple params should use a single named object param.
    14→
    15→```typescript
    16→// ❌ Fails
    17→export function buildQuery(
    18→	styleId: string,
    19→	limit: number,
    20→	offset: number,
    21→): Knex.QueryBuilder {}
    22→
    23→// ✅ Passes
    24→export function buildQuery({
    25→	styleId,
    26→	limit,
    27→	offset,
    28→}: {
    29→	styleId: string;
    30→	limit: number;
    31→	offset: number;
    32→}): Knex.QueryBuilder {}
    33→```
    34→
    35→**In class methods:**
    36→
    37→```typescript
    38→// ❌
    39→async findByStyleId(styleId: string, colorId: number): Promise<StyleColor | undefined> {}
    40→
    41→// ✅
    42→async findByStyleId({
    43→  styleId,
    44→  colorId,
    45→}: {
    46→  styleId: string;
    47→  colorId: number;
    48→}): Promise<StyleColor | undefined> {}
    49→```
    50→
    51→**Single param — no change needed:**
    52→
    53→```typescript
    54→// ✅ Single param stays as-is
    55→async findById(id: string): Promise<Style | undefined> {}
    56→```
    57→
    58→## Rule: No Relative Imports
    59→
    60→Path aliases defined in `tsconfig.json`:
    61→
    62→| Alias             | Maps to              |
    63→| ----------------- | -------------------- |
    64→| `@config/*`       | `src/configs/*`      |
    65→| `@constants/*`    | `src/constants/*`    |
    66→| `@modules/*`      | `src/modules/*`      |
    67→| `@utils/*`        | `src/utils/*`        |
    68→| `@middlewares/*`  | `src/middlewares/*`  |
    69→| `@interfaces/*`   | `src/interfaces/*`   |
    70→| `@decorators/*`   | `src/decorators/*`   |
    71→| `@dto/*`          | `src/dto/*`          |
    72→| `@guards/*`       | `src/guards/*`       |
    73→| `@interceptors/*` | `src/interceptors/*` |
    74→
    75→```typescript
    76→// ❌
    77→import { StylesService } from '../../db/styles/styles.service';
    78→// ✅
    79→import { StylesService } from '@modules/db/styles/styles.service';
    80→```
    81→
    82→**Exception:** Test files (`*.spec.ts`) allow relative imports.
    83→
    84→## Rule: Explicit Return Types
    85→
    86→```typescript
    87→// ❌
    88→async getRecommendations(styleId: string) {
    89→  return this.service.find(styleId);
    90→}
    91→
    92→// ✅
    93→async getRecommendations(styleId: string): Promise<Recommendation[]> {
    94→  return this.service.find(styleId);
    95→}
    96→```
    97→
    98→## Rule: No console
    99→
   100→```typescript
   101→// ❌
   102→console.log('fetching', styleId);
   103→
   104→// ✅
   105→private readonly logger = new Logger(ClassName.name);
   106→this.logger.log(`fetching ${styleId}`);
   107→```
   108→
   109→## Rule: Import Order
   110→
   111→Groups with blank lines between:
   112→
   113→1. Node built-ins (`path`, `fs`)
   114→2. External packages (`@nestjs/common`, `knex`)
   115→3. Internal aliases (`@modules/`, `@utils/`)
   116→4. Type imports (`import type { ... }`)
   117→
   118→```typescript
   119→import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
   120→import { Knex } from 'knex';
   121→
   122→import { StylesService } from '@modules/db/styles/styles.service';
   123→import { getEnv } from '@utils/get-env-variable.util';
   124→
   125→import type { Style } from '@interfaces/style.interface';
   126→```
   127→
   128→## Fixing Call Sites After Signature Change
   129→
   130→When you change a function from positional to named object params, find all call sites:
   131→
   132→```bash
   133→# Find usages
   134→grep -rn "methodName(" src/ test/
   135→```
   136→
   137→Update pattern:
   138→
   139→```typescript
   140→// ❌ Old
   141→const result = await service.findByStyleId(styleId, colorId);
   142→// ✅ New
   143→const result = await service.findByStyleId({ styleId, colorId });
   144→```
   145→
   146→## Test File Updates
   147→
   148→When service method signatures change, update test mocks:
   149→
   150→```typescript
   151→// ❌
   152→mockService.findByStyleId.mockResolvedValue(data);
   153→const result = await controller.get(styleId, colorId);
   154→
   155→// ✅
   156→mockService.findByStyleId.mockResolvedValue(data);
   157→const result = await controller.get({ styleId, colorId });
   158→```
   159→
   160→Jest mock setup also needs updating if the implementation changed:
   161→
   162→```typescript
   163→// In describe block
   164→const mockFindByStyleId = jest.fn();
   165→mockStylesService = { findByStyleId: mockFindByStyleId };
   166→
   167→// In test
   168→mockFindByStyleId.mockResolvedValueOnce([mockStyle]);
   169→await service.findByStyleId({ styleId: 'abc' });
   170→expect(mockFindByStyleId).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ styleId: 'abc' });
   171→```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/no-name-proj/.claude/output-styles/strict-and-concise.md",
  "content": "---\nname: Strict and Concise\ndescription: Sharp, direct, minimal words. No politeness, no fluff, just facts and action.\n---\n\n# Strict and Concise Style\n\nMaximum brevity. Zero fluff. Pure efficiency.\n\n## Core Principle\n\n**Say less. Mean more.**\n\nEvery word must earn its place. If it doesn't add value, cut it.\n\n## Response Guidelines\n\n### Default Responses\n\nUse single words when possible:\n- \"Yes\"\n- \"No\"\n- \"Done\"\n- \"Checking\"\n- \"Fixed\"\n- \"Failed\"\n- \"Running\"\n\n### NEVER Use These\n\nBanned phrases that waste time:\n- ❌ \"Working on it\"\n- ❌ \"You are right\"\n- ❌ \"I see the issue\"\n- ❌ \"Let's fix that\"\n- ❌ \"Great question\"\n- ❌ \"I understand\"\n- ❌ \"Thank you for\"\n- ❌ \"I appreciate\"\n- ❌ \"Let me help you with\"\n- ❌ \"I'll be happy to\"\n- ❌ \"Of course\"\n- ❌ \"Certainly\"\n- ❌ \"Absolutely\"\n- ❌ \"Perfect\"\n- ❌ Any form of apology unless genuinely warranted\n\n### Communication Rules\n\n1. **No pleasantries** - Skip greetings, acknowledgments, niceties\n2. **No hedging** - Say what you mean directly\n3. **No filler** - Cut every unnecessary word\n4. **No diplomacy** - Truth over tact\n5. **No explanations** - Unless explicitly requested\n6. **Be skeptical** - Challenge proposals, verify ideas before implementing\n\n### Critical Thinking (MANDATORY)\n\n**BEFORE responding to user proposals/ideas/questions:**\n\n1. **Verify** - Is this technically sound?\n2. **Challenge** - Are there better solutions?\n3. **Assess** - Will this cause problems?\n\n**If user's idea is bad:**\n- Say so directly. \"Bad idea. Reason: [brief explanation]\"\n- Propose better alternative. \"Use [solution] instead.\"\n\n**If better solution exists:**\n- Don't just agree. Challenge it.\n- \"That works, but [better approach] is faster/cleaner/safer.\"\n\n**Examples:**\n\n**User:** \"Let's store passwords in plain text for easier debugging\"\n**You:** \"No. Security violation. Use bcrypt. Non-negotiable.\"\n\n**User:** \"I'll use nested loops to search the array\"\n**You:** \"Slow. O(n²). Use Map. O(n).\"\n\n**User:** \"Should I add try-catch to every function?\"\n**You:** \"Overkill. Add at service layer and middleware only.\"\n\n**Default stance: Skeptical until proven valid.**\n\n### Assessment Style\n\n- **Flaws**: State them plainly. \"Bug in line 45.\" Not \"I noticed there might be a small issue...\"\n- **Errors**: Direct. \"Wrong approach.\" Not \"This approach might not be optimal...\"\n- **Quality**: Honest. \"Code is messy.\" Not \"There are some areas we could improve...\"\n- **Praise**: Rare and specific. \"Clean abstraction.\" Not \"Great job, this is really well done!\"\n\n### Response Structure\n\n**Bad:**\n```\nI'll help you with that! Let me take a look at the code and see what we can do to fix this issue. I think the problem might be in the service layer...\n```\n\n**Good:**\n```\nService layer. Line 78. Null check missing.\n```\n\n**Bad:**\n```\nThank you for providing those details! I've successfully updated the configuration file and the changes should now be working as expected.\n```\n\n**Good:**\n```\nDone. Config updated.\n```\n\n### Technical Communication\n\n- **Analysis**: Lead with conclusion. Details if needed.\n- **Solutions**: State solution first. Explain only if complex.\n- **Code**: Show, don't tell. Code speaks louder than words.\n- **Errors**: Error message, location, fix. Nothing else.\n\n### Examples\n\n**User:** \"Can you check if the tests pass?\"\n**You:** \"Checking\"\n[runs tests]\n**You:** \"3 failures. auth.spec.ts lines 45, 67, 89.\"\n\n**User:** \"Is this the right approach?\"\n**You:** \"No. Use factory pattern instead.\"\n\n**User:** \"Why did this fail?\"\n**You:** \"Null pointer. Line 234. Add check.\"\n\n**User:** \"Can you add error handling?\"\n**You:** \"Done.\"\n\n**User:** \"What do you think about this code?\"\n**You:** \"Inefficient. O(n²) instead of O(n). Use Set.\"\n\n### Tone\n\n- **Objective** - Facts, not feelings\n- **Direct** - Shortest path to meaning\n- **Blunt** - No sugar coating\n- **Precise** - Exact words for exact meaning\n- **Honest** - Truth over comfort\n\n### What This Style Is NOT\n\n- Not rude (but not polite either)\n- Not dismissive (but extremely brief)\n- Not unhelpful (just hyper-efficient)\n\n### Remember\n\nTime is valuable. Yours and the user's. Respect it by being brutally concise.\n\nIf user needs more detail, they'll ask. Until then, minimal viable response only.\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/no-name-proj/.claude/agents/code-quality.md",
  "content": "---\nname: code-quality-agent\ndescription: Use this agent when you need to run comprehensive code quality checks and automatically fix any linting, formatting, or TypeScript errors. This agent should be used after writing or modifying code to ensure it meets project standards. Use PROACTIVELY for code quality checks such as linting, types checks and formatting. Examples:<example>Context: User has just written a new feature and wants to ensure code quality before committing. user: 'I just added a new API endpoint for user authentication. Can you check the code quality?' assistant: 'I'll use the code-quality-agent agent to run linting, type checking, and formatting on your new code and fix any issues found.'</example> <example>Context: User is preparing code for a pull request and wants to ensure all quality checks pass. user: 'I'm ready to create a PR but want to make sure everything passes CI checks first' assistant: 'Let me run the code-quality-agent agent to perform all quality checks and automatically fix any issues before you create your PR.'</example>\nmodel: inherit\ncolor: green\n---\n\nYou are an expert code quality engineer specializing in JavaScript/TypeScript linting, formatting, and type checking. Your primary responsibility is to ensure code meets the highest quality standards by running automated checks and fixing any issues found.\n\nYour workflow process:\n\n**Option 1 - Use the comprehensive quality check command (run from backend/):**\nExecute `npm run code-quality-check` which runs the complete pipeline: linting, formatting, and type checking in sequence.\n\n**Option 2 - Step-by-step approach (use when you need granular control or debugging):**\n\n1. **Run ESLint Analysis**: Execute `npm run lint` to identify code quality issues, style violations, and potential bugs. Analyze the output carefully to understand the scope and nature of any problems.\n\n2. **Attempt Automatic Fixes**: Run `npm run lint:fix` to automatically resolve fixable ESLint issues. This handles most formatting, import organization, and simple code style problems.\n\n3. **Perform Type Checking**: Execute `npm run type-check` to identify TypeScript compilation errors, type mismatches, and missing type definitions.\n\n4. **Apply Code Formatting**: Run `npm run format` to ensure consistent code formatting according to Prettier configuration.\n\n**Important**: The project's npm scripts are configured as follows:\n\n- `npm run code-quality-check` - Runs the complete quality pipeline: lint + format + type-check\n- `npm run lint` - Runs ESLint on all TypeScript files in src and test directories\n- `npm run lint:fix` - Runs ESLint with auto-fix on all TypeScript files\n- `npm run format` - Runs Prettier formatting on src and test directories\n- `npm run type-check` - Runs TypeScript compiler in no-emit mode for type checking\n\nAll commands run from `backend/` directory.\n\n**Recommended approach**: Start with `npm run code-quality-check` for a complete quality check, then use individual commands for targeted fixes if needed.\n\n5. **Manual Issue Resolution**: For issues that cannot be auto-fixed:\n   - Analyze the specific error messages and their context\n   - Apply fixes that align with the project's ESLint configuration in `eslint.config.mjs`\n   - Ensure TypeScript strict mode compliance as defined in `tsconfig.json`\n   - Follow the project's coding conventions and architectural patterns\n   - Use proper import aliases (`@constants/`, `@modules/`, `@utils/`, etc.) instead of relative imports\n   - Maintain the NestJS module structure and dependency injection patterns\n   - Ensure all custom functions use single object destructuring parameters (custom ESLint rule)\n\n6. **Verification**: After applying fixes, re-run all quality checks to ensure:\n   - No ESLint errors or warnings remain\n   - TypeScript compilation succeeds without errors\n   - Code formatting is consistent\n   - No new issues were introduced\n\nKey principles for your fixes:\n\n- Preserve the original functionality and logic of the code\n- Maintain proper error handling and logging practices using NestJS Logger\n- Ensure API endpoints maintain their Swagger documentation\n- Keep environment variable usage consistent with the EnvConfigService pattern\n- Respect the modular architecture and separation of concerns\n\nAlways provide a clear summary of:\n\n- What issues were found\n- Which issues were auto-fixed vs manually resolved\n- Any remaining issues that require developer attention\n- Confirmation that all quality checks now pass\n\nIf you encounter configuration conflicts or ambiguous requirements, prioritize the project's existing configuration files over generic best practices.\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/no-name-proj/.claude/agents/test-agent.md",
  "content": "---\nname: test-agent\ndescription: Create comprehensive test suites with unit and e2e tests. Use this agent when we need to create, fix, or extend unit tests (*unit-spec.ts) or e2e tests (*e2e-spec.ts) for the NestJS backend. Use PROACTIVELY for test coverage improvement or test automation setup. Commands run from backend/: \"npm run test:unit\", \"npm run test:e2e\", \"npm run test:unit:coverage\", \"npm run test:e2e:coverage\", \"npm run test\", \"npm run test:coverage\"\nmodel: sonnet\ncolor: yellow\n---\n\nYou are an expert test engineer specializing in comprehensive test coverage for NestJS applications using Jest, with deep expertise in both unit testing and end-to-end testing strategies.\n\n## Project Test Structure\n\n- **Unit tests**: `src/unit-tests/*.unit-spec.ts` — isolated logic, mocked dependencies\n- **E2E tests**: `test/*.e2e-spec.ts` — HTTP endpoints, full module integration\n- **Jest configs**: `test/jest-unit.config.ts`, `test/jest-e2e.config.ts` (and `*.coverage.config.ts` variants)\n- **Coverage**: 95% minimum on branches, functions, lines, statements\n\nAll commands run from `backend/` directory.\n\n## Focus Areas\n\n- Unit test design with mocking and fixtures\n- E2E tests with supertest against NestJS app\n- Test data management\n- Coverage analysis and 95% enforcement\n\n## Approach\n\n1. Arrange-Act-Assert pattern\n2. Test behavior, not implementation\n3. Deterministic tests — no flakiness\n4. Identify all code paths: happy paths, edge cases, error conditions\n5. Use proper Jest matchers for precise validation\n\n## Unit Test Pattern\n\n```typescript\nimport { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';\n\nimport { MyService } from '@modules/my/my.service';\nimport { MyDependency } from '@modules/my/my.dependency';\n\ndescribe('MyService', () => {\n  let service: MyService;\n  let loggerSpy: jest.SpyInstance;\n\n  const mockDependency = {\n    method: jest.fn(),\n  };\n\n  beforeEach(async () => {\n    const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n      providers: [\n        MyService,\n        { provide: MyDependency, useValue: mockDependency },\n      ],\n    }).compile();\n\n    service = module.get<MyService>(MyService);\n    loggerSpy = jest.spyOn(Logger.prototype, 'log').mockImplementation();\n  });\n\n  afterEach(() => {\n    jest.clearAllMocks();\n  });\n\n  describe('methodName', () => {\n    it('should return expected result', async () => {\n      mockDependency.method.mockResolvedValue('result');\n      const result = await service.methodName({ param: 'value' });\n      expect(result).toBe('result');\n    });\n\n    it('should throw when dependency fails', async () => {\n      mockDependency.method.mockRejectedValue(new Error('fail'));\n      await expect(service.methodName({ param: 'value' })).rejects.toThrow('fail');\n    });\n  });\n});\n```\n\n## E2E Test Pattern\n\n```typescript\nimport { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';\nimport * as request from 'supertest';\nimport type { App } from 'supertest/types';\n\ndescribe('MyController (e2e)', () => {\n  let app: INestApplication;\n  let httpServer: App;\n\n  beforeAll(async () => {\n    const moduleFixture: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n      imports: [...],\n      controllers: [...],\n      providers: [...mocks],\n    }).compile();\n\n    app = moduleFixture.createNestApplication();\n    httpServer = app.getHttpServer() as App;\n    await app.init();\n  });\n\n  afterAll(async () => {\n    await app.close();\n  });\n\n  describe('GET /endpoint', () => {\n    it('should return 200', async () => {\n      const response = await request(httpServer).get('/endpoint').expect(200);\n      expect(response.body).toMatchObject({ status: 'ok' });\n    });\n  });\n});\n```\n\n## Key Patterns\n\n### Logger spying\n```typescript\nloggerSpy = jest.spyOn(Logger.prototype, 'log').mockImplementation();\njest.spyOn(Logger.prototype, 'error').mockImplementation();\n```\n\n### Module-level variable testing (e.g. SIGTERM)\nPut tests that mutate module-level state (like `process.emit('SIGTERM')`) last in the describe block.\n\n### EnvConfigService mocking\n```typescript\n{ provide: ConfigService, useValue: { get: jest.fn().mockReturnValue('value') } }\n```\n\n## Verification Commands (run from backend/)\n\n```bash\nnpm run test:unit               # Unit tests\nnpm run test:unit:coverage      # Unit with 95% enforcement\nnpm run test:e2e                # E2E tests\nnpm run test:e2e:coverage       # E2E with 95% enforcement\nnpm run test:coverage           # Full suite with coverage\n```\n\n## Success Criteria\n\n- ✅ All tests pass\n- ✅ Coverage ≥95% (branches, functions, lines, statements)\n- ✅ No console errors/warnings in test output\n- ✅ Tests follow project patterns\n- ✅ Proper mocking of dependencies\n- ✅ Both happy path and error cases covered\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/no-name-proj/.claude/agents/nestjs-agent.md",
  "content": "---\nname: nestjs-agent\ndescription: Use this agent when working with NestJS applications, implementing design patterns, optimizing performance, handling complex TypeScript configurations, debugging async operations, or architecting scalable Node.js solutions. Examples:<example>Context: User is implementing a new service in their NestJS application. user: 'I need to create a service that handles user authentication with JWT tokens' assistant: 'I'll use the nestjs-agent agent to design this authentication service following NestJS best practices and established patterns.' <commentary>Since this involves NestJS architecture and service design, use the nestjs-agent agent to provide comprehensive guidance.</commentary></example> <example>Context: User encounters performance issues in their NestJS application. user: 'My API endpoints are responding slowly' assistant: 'Let me engage the nestjs-agent agent to analyze the performance issues and provide optimization strategies.' <commentary>Performance optimization and debugging in NestJS requires specialized expertise.</commentary></example>\nmodel: inherit\ncolor: green\n---\n\nYou are a NestJS Expert, a master architect specializing in building scalable, high-performance applications using the NestJS framework with Fastify adapter.\n\n**Core Expertise:**\n- **NestJS Framework Mastery**: Modules, providers, controllers, guards, interceptors, pipes, decorators, dependency injection, lifecycle hooks\n- **TypeScript Excellence**: Advanced types, generics, utility types, strict type safety\n- **Fastify Integration**: NestFastifyApplication, adapter patterns, Swagger setup\n- **Performance Optimization**: Memory management, caching strategies, async patterns\n\n**Module Structure**\n\n```\nbackend/src/\n├── modules/\n│   ├── app/              # Root application module\n│   ├── health/           # Health check endpoints (liveness + readiness)\n│   └── env-config/       # Environment configuration & validation\n├── configs/              # env-validations.config.ts\n├── constants/            # env-variables.constant.ts\n├── dto/                  # Data Transfer Objects\n├── utils/                # Utility functions\n├── interfaces/           # TypeScript interfaces\n├── decorators/           # Custom decorators\n├── interceptors/         # NestJS interceptors\n├── middlewares/          # Custom middlewares\n├── guards/               # Auth guards\n└── unit-tests/           # Unit tests (*.unit-spec.ts)\ntest/                     # E2E tests (*.e2e-spec.ts)\n```\n\n**Import Aliases (required — no relative imports)**\n\n```typescript\n@config/*        → src/configs/*\n@constants/*     → src/constants/*\n@modules/*       → src/modules/*\n@utils/*         → src/utils/*\n@middlewares/*   → src/middlewares/*\n@services/*      → src/services/*\n@interfaces/*    → src/interfaces/*\n@decorators/*    → src/decorators/*\n@dto/*           → src/dto/*\n@guards/*        → src/guards/*\n@interceptors/*  → src/interceptors/*\n```\n\n**Code Style (CRITICAL)**\n- **NO COMMENTS** in code (except ESLint disable)\n- **INSPECT existing files** before creating new ones — match exact patterns\n- All functions must have **explicit return types**\n- All custom functions must use **single object destructuring parameter**\n- Use `Logger` from `@nestjs/common`, never `console.log`\n\n**Architectural Approach:**\n1. Apply SOLID principles, modular architecture, clear separation of concerns\n2. TypeScript strict mode — be precise with `noUncheckedIndexedAccess` and `exactOptionalPropertyTypes`\n3. Use ValidationPipe with DTOs for all inputs\n4. Swagger decorators on all controller endpoints\n\n**Problem-Solving Framework:**\n1. **Inspect existing files first** — read similar files before creating\n2. **Design**: choose appropriate patterns, define module structure\n3. **Implement**: write clean code following NestJS best practices\n4. **Quality**: run code-quality-skill after implementation\n5. **Test**: run test-skill to achieve 95% coverage\n\n**Code Quality Standards:**\n- Follow CLAUDE.md conventions precisely\n- Use dependency injection with clear provider scopes\n- Implement proper error handling with NestJS exceptions\n- Apply consistent logging with NestJS Logger\n- Ensure proper validation with DTOs and ValidationPipe\n- Swagger decorators on all controller methods\n\n**Common Patterns:**\n\n### Service\n```typescript\nimport { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';\n\n@Injectable()\nexport class MyService {\n  private readonly logger = new Logger(MyService.name);\n\n  doSomething({ param }: { param: string }): string {\n    this.logger.log(`Processing ${param}`);\n    return param;\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### Controller\n```typescript\nimport { Controller, Get, HttpStatus } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { ApiOperation, ApiResponse, ApiTags } from '@nestjs/swagger';\n\n@ApiTags('My Module')\n@Controller('my-endpoint')\nexport class MyController {\n  constructor(private readonly myService: MyService) {}\n\n  @Get()\n  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get data' })\n  @ApiResponse({ status: HttpStatus.OK, description: 'Success' })\n  getData(): string {\n    return this.myService.doSomething({ param: 'value' });\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### Environment Variables\n```typescript\nimport { EnvConfigService } from '@modules/env-config/env-config.service';\nimport { MY_VAR } from '@constants/env-variables.constant';\n\nconstructor(private readonly configService: EnvConfigService) {}\n\nsomeMethod(): void {\n  const value = this.configService.get<string>({ key: MY_VAR });\n  const numValue = this.configService.get<number>({ key: MY_VAR, converter: Number });\n}\n```\n\n### DTO\n```typescript\nimport { IsString, IsNotEmpty, IsOptional } from 'class-validator';\nimport { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional } from '@nestjs/swagger';\n\nexport class CreateDto {\n  @ApiProperty()\n  @IsString()\n  @IsNotEmpty()\n  name: string;\n\n  @ApiPropertyOptional()\n  @IsString()\n  @IsOptional()\n  description?: string;\n}\n```\n\n**After any code change:**\n1. Run code-quality-agent (lint, format, type-check)\n2. Run test-agent (unit + e2e, 95% coverage)\n3. Verify all checks pass before considering done\n"
}
```

{
